科技股漲多修正，資金輪動主導盤勢
2026 年 8 月
7 月全球股市呈現鮮明的區域輪動 ， 資金由先前漲幅集中的亞洲AI 供應鏈 ， 轉向估值與今年以來
表現相對落後的市場。以美元計價觀察， MSCI 中國、東協及拉丁美洲為當月前三強。相較之下，
MSCI 新興亞洲與新興市場跌幅較大，MSCI 新興亞洲中南韓與台灣合計權重接近六成，7 月南韓半
導體及 AI 硬體股因估值偏高 、 資本支出回收疑慮與槓桿部位平倉而急跌 ， 成為指數的主要壓力來源。
即便 7 月新興亞洲與新興市場出現回檔，但年初迄今表現仍佳，反觀中國與印度仍分別下跌 8.0%及

9.0%。因此，我們認為 7 月盤勢較接近強勢 AI 題材暫時性獲利了結，而先前落後市場低基期補漲的
資金輪動行情，尚不足以判定全球股市原有多頭結構已全面反轉。
七月全球債市整體偏弱，僅新興當地債及亞洲高收益債維持正報酬。以美元計價觀察，新興美元
債下跌 1.30% 為最弱券種。當月債市壓力主要來自能源價格波動、通膨黏著性及聯準會政策不確定
性。Fed 於 7/29 決議將利率維持在 3.50%至 3.75%，但三位地區聯準銀行總裁主張立即升息一碼，
官方聲明亦明確指出通膨仍高於 2%目標，能源供給衝擊持續推升部分價格。當日會後 30 年期美債
殖利率創 2007 年以來高位，但 2 年期殖利率卻下降，反映市場並非單純押注短期升息，而是提高對
長期通膨、政策可信度與期限溢酬的要求，殖利率曲線因而明顯陡峭化。
我們認為，七月股市修正主要來自折現率、評價與籌碼調整，目前尚未出現AI 需求下降的證據。
半導體類股近期修正後，評價壓力已部分釋放，但技術面仍未形成明確底部。既有核心持股不宜因短
線波動而全面減碼，但建議降低高評價、高擁擠度及高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集中度。此外，投資人

可以定時定額或在拉回時分批布局具獲利潛力的台、日股市。韓國股市則受三星電子、SK 海力士雙
巨頭合計占指數權重逾半，個股風險易被結構性放大，建議投資人於短線反彈適度調節相關曝險。
在聯準會減少前瞻指引、利率路徑更依賴數據環境下，市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將提
高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較大
波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給
增加等負面因素。因此，建議債券布局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸
引力的息收，亦能降低短端政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

美國股市：AI 成長主線未變，短線估值風險升溫
七月美股受伊朗戰爭不確定性、 聯準會態度轉鷹干擾 ，股市出現巨幅震盪 。 雖財報盈餘表現強勁，
但市場開始觀察資本支出擴張壓迫科技龍頭企業的現金流。指數上揚主要由微軟、蘋果、亞馬遜、輝
達、埃克森美孚、博通、摩根大通銀行、雪佛龍、萬事達卡、威士卡等企業帶動，表現相對弱勢的族
群包括美光、特斯拉、英特爾、應用材料、科林研發、科磊、晟碟、超微半導體、卡特彼勒、康寧等。
美國經濟整體仍維持穩健 ， 但第二季GDP 年化季增率由 2.1%放緩至 1.5%， 主要反映進口增加、
企業庫存調整及政府支出下降。民間消費仍是第二季經濟成長的主要支撐，年化季增率由第一季的

0.5%大幅回升至 3.2%，其中耐久財消費成長 6.8%、非耐久財成長 4.4%，服務消費亦由 0.5%回升
至 2.2%。此外，第二季民間投資成長3.0%，其中非住宅投資成長 8.4%，顯示 AI 資料中心、軟體與
研發支出仍持續擴張。另一方面，第二季進口大幅成長11.5%，增幅主要集中於電信設備、半導體及
工業設備等資本財，反映企業資本支出需求仍相當強勁。
能源價格回落有助通膨降溫，但整體物價壓力仍不容忽視。6 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 月增率同步放
緩 ， 其中整體PCE 轉為月減 0.1%， 反映能源價格明顯回落 ， 足以抵銷其他商品與服務價格上漲壓力，
年增率為 3.7%，仍高於 Fed 目標。核心 PCE 月增率亦降至 0.1%，創近數月低點；其中，聯準會高
度關注的超級核心服務通膨出現降溫 ， 主要受投資組合管理費漲幅由4.5%大幅降至 0.6%， 以及旅館
住宿與機票價格回落帶動。不過，醫療保健價格仍維持穩健增長，且部分商品價格持續受到關稅影響
而上揚，加上美伊戰事仍未落幕，通膨風險依舊存在。

本輪由 AI 投資帶動的「AI 通膨」 ，對整體CPI 的直接推升有限，卻有助於川普推動「美國製造」
政策目標。2024 年以來 ， 全球雲端服務業者大幅擴張AI 資本支出 ， 帶動高階晶片 、記憶體需求急升，
不過，電視、電腦周邊及手機合計占美國CPI 權重偏低，即使相關產品價格上漲，對整體通膨的直接
影響仍相對有限。相較之下，AI 通膨對產業結構與供應鏈在地化的影響更值得關注。台積電毛利率
上升同時也擴大在美國資本支出 、美光也在記憶體價格回升後重新啟動紐約廠建設、輝達則進一步投
資光通訊與先進封裝業者 ， 協助相關產能在美國落地 。 唯有當價格與需求長期維持在足以支撐獲利的
水準，企業才有能力承擔高額資本支出，並推動製造業供應鏈回美擴張。
目前標普 500 指數續創新高，本益比已升至 25.8 倍、股價淨值比達 5.8 倍，短期評價偏高。雖
然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但AI 技術與競爭格局變化快速，領先廠商尚未與後續追
趕者明顯拉開差距。短期仍需留意市場過熱與估值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：經濟溫和復甦，評價仍具吸引力
七月歐股受伊朗戰爭不確定性干擾而震盪。泛歐 600 指數中，能源與金融股表現相對強勢，包括
殼牌、匯豐、道達爾能源、BP、畢爾巴鄂比斯開銀行、聯合聖保羅銀行及法國巴黎銀行等；相較之
下 ， 半導體 、 科技硬體與部分防禦型消費股表現偏弱 ， 包括艾司摩爾 、 英飛凌 、 意法半導體、諾基亞、
雀巢等。整體市場焦點由科技股回歸能源與金融族群。
歐元區第 2 季 GDP 初值季增率回升至 0.4%，顯示經濟動能在第 1 季停滯後逐步修復。不過，成
員國表現仍明顯分化：西班牙季增 0.7%，持續領先主要經濟體，主要受惠於服務業復甦與觀光需求

回升；德國 GDP 季增率則放緩至 0.2%，反映製造業疲弱與出口需求不足仍限制復甦力道；法國則
由前季萎縮轉為成長 0.2%，顯示內需逐步改善。整體而言，第 2 季 GDP 回升主要受消費改善、投
資回穩及政府支出支撐，但復甦力道仍屬溫和。
7 月歐元區通膨率受能源價格反彈影響 ， 小幅回升至2.9%， 已連續5 個月高於 ECB 的 2.0%目標 ，
但仍低於 5 月 3.2%的高點。核心通膨則升至 2.5%，主要受服務業與非能源工業品價格同步走揚帶
動，其中服務業通膨由3.2%升至 3.3%，非能源工業品由 0.7%升至 0.9%；食品與菸酒年增率則續降
至 1.2%，反映食品價格降溫趨勢延續。整體而言，物價壓力僅溫和回升，尚未出現全面加速跡象。
ECB 在七月利率決策會議維持政策利率 2.25%不變，重申將採取逐次會議、數據依賴的決策模
式，不預先承諾未來利率路徑，官方仍維持經濟成長風險偏下行的評估。雖然歐元區整體通膨較前期
高點放緩 ， 但中東衝突再度升溫 ， 推升原油與天然氣價格 ， 加上目前全球能源與天然氣庫存水位偏低 ，

未來能源價格變化及其對通膨的傳導效果，仍將是市場關注重點。ECB 刻意未恢復前瞻指引，並持續
強調後續數據的重要性 ， 反映其希望在高度不確定環境下保留政策彈性 ， 因此不排除9 月升息的可能 。
7 月歐元區綜合 PMI 回升至 51.9，重返榮枯線之上並創 5 個月新高，整體景氣動能有所改善。
其中，服務業 PMI 升至 51.6，受惠於需求回升而重返擴張區間；製造業 PMI 亦由 51.4 升至 51.9，
加上新訂單 5 個月來首度恢復成長，進一步支撐景氣修復。
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦 ， 泛歐600 指數成分股多為具備全球布局優勢的產業龍頭 。 目前指數
本益比約 17.5 倍、股價淨值比約 2.5 倍，評價仍屬合理。雖然能源價格高檔與通膨再升溫風險仍需
留意，但在評價合理、伊朗戰爭有望於第三季落幕的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

日本股市：日圓匯率短線急升，股市評價合理
七月日股受地緣政治風險與全球科技股回檔影響，日經 225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，金融、外銷
與大型權值股表現相對強勢，包括三菱 UFJ 金融集團、日立製作所、索尼、豐田、三井住友金融、
東京海上控股、瑞穗金融、三菱商事等；半導體與電子零組件族群則相對承壓，包括東京威力科創、
村田製作所、鎧俠、瑞薩、Disco、信越化學等。整體市場焦點由科技股轉向外銷股與升息預期受惠
的金融股。
7 月東京 CPI 年增率自前月的 1.7%攀升至 2.0%、東京核心 CPI（扣除生鮮食品） 年增率為1.9%、

扣除食品與能源後的 CPI 年增率則為 1.3%。商品價格年增率由前月的 2.3%升至 2.9%，是通膨回升
的主要原因；服務價格年增率則小幅升至 1.2%，顯示薪資成長仍推升服務價格。分項來看，本月通
膨上行主要來自能源與公用事業費用，反映電費與瓦斯費價格上漲；同時，食品價格年增率也由前月
的 3.8%回升至 4.0%。展望後市，日本政府 7~9 月重啟電費補貼，將抑制能源價格。不過，近期國
際油價再度攀高，可能抵消部分政策效果。
日本央行七月利率決策會議維持政策利率不變，委員以 8 比 1 同意將政策利率維持於 1.0%。僅
高田創委員主張將政策利率調高至 1.25%。就景氣現況來看，日本經濟仍呈現溫和復甦態勢，儘管部
分經濟活動受到中東局勢影響而略顯疲弱，但出口與工業生產大致保持平穩。受惠於全球 AI 相關需
求強勁成長，企業固定投資仍溫和增加；同時，私人消費在就業與所得環境改善支撐下仍具韌性。日
銀也將 2026 財年 GDP 年增率預估由前次的 0.5%小幅上調至 0.6%，2027 財年由 0.7%上調至 0.8%。

近期美日聯手干預日圓匯率，日本財務大臣片山皋月表示，已協同美國財政部實施買進日圓的
干預措施，以因應近來日圓匯率過度波動及失序走勢，且不排除進一步採取協同干預。美國財長貝森
特也表示，將毫不猶豫參與進一步的聯合日圓干預行動，並強烈支持日本採取果斷措施，修正日圓嚴
重低估的問題。美國總統川普則稱，日本需要一些幫助。此次為日本與美國 15 年來首次就日圓採取
聯合行動，兩國共同干預匯市確實對日圓形成支撐，突襲式干預也有助引發投資人空單回補，暫時延
續日圓升值趨勢。不過，目前日美政策利率差距仍高達 2.5%，若日本央行無法加快升息步伐，中長
期匯價重新回歸利差基本面，日圓仍可能重拾偏弱格局。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材 、 高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政策利多支撐，
儘管指數已明顯上漲，目前本益比約 19.5 倍、股價淨值比約 1.8 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震
盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：AI 出口與接單動能續強，8 月聚焦財報兌現與供應鏈擴散
7 月台股因前期漲幅較大、科技股評價偏高，加上市場重新檢視AI 投資回收期、企業獲利能否符
合高預期，外資調節、融資去化與槓桿資金停損進一步放大波動，資金也由高評價電子股轉向較具防
禦性的族群。不過，這波修正有助消化過高評價與浮動籌碼，降低市場過熱風險；後續隨雲端業者財
報持續驗證 AI 資本支出，半導體、伺服器及先進封裝需求延續，市場焦點可望逐步回歸企業獲利與
產業基本面 。6 月景氣對策信號升至 41 分 、 連續第7 個月亮紅燈 ， 製造業銷售與加班工時同步轉強，

顯示 AI 動能已由出口延伸至生產、設備投資與勞動投入。雖然產業間景氣感受仍有落差，但下半年
電子產品備貨需求、半導體資本支出及 AI 基礎建設持續推進，仍可望支撐出口與企業獲利，為後續
盤勢提供正向動能。
當前臺灣經濟正處於生產擴張的黃金期。最新公布的總體經濟數據與半導體龍頭營收呈現全面共
振，印證全球 AI 資本支出（CapEx）並非流於故事題材，而是實質轉化為台廠的訂單與獲利。儘管
全球金融市場短期受到地緣政治干擾與美債殖利率波動影響 ， 引發台股在高檔震盪 ， 但主計總處大幅
上修 2026 年 GDP 預測至 9.64%。國發會公布 6 月景氣對策信號綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較 5 月
增加 2 分，連續第 7 個月呈現代表景氣熱絡的紅燈，為史上第二長的連續紅燈紀錄，顯示台灣景氣
並非單月跳升，而是已形成一段相對持續的高檔擴張週期。
展望 8 月，台灣景氣仍有望維持高檔，但景氣分數的邊際變化將比燈號本身更值得關注。全球
AI 基礎建設持續擴張，加上下半年進入消費性電子備貨旺季，預期有利半導體、AI 伺服器、先進封

裝、散熱、電源及高速傳輸供應鏈；國內主要半導體業者提高資本支出，也將持續帶動設備、材料及
廠務工程需求。另一方面，景氣分數已接近紅燈區間上緣 45 分，未來即使出口與生產維持強勁，受
到比較基期墊高影響，分數未必持續上升，因此不宜將分數持平或小幅回落直接解讀為景氣反轉。
投資策略上可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但選股宜從題材轉向獲利品質。除先進製程外，8
月可提高對先進封裝、測試介面、AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、及記憶體的關注。IDC 預估 2026 年全球半
導體市場規模將達 1.29 兆美元、年增52.8%，資料中心半導體營收達 4,771 億美元，意味著AI 投資
正由 GPU 延伸至記憶體、網路與系統級零組件。不過，若股價已提前反映高成長，8 月仍應留意財
測不及預期、匯率、成本上升及資金集中度過高所引發的波動。

中國股市：復甦力道偏緩，科技自主為結構性題材但仍待驗證
7 月中國經濟延續 「總量放緩 、 科技製造相對抗跌」 的分化格局 。2026 年上半年 GDP 年增 4.7%，
第二季成長率由第一季的 5.0%放緩至 4.3%；6 月規模以上工業增加值年增 5.3%，其中製造業成長
6.0%，電腦、通信及其他電子設備製造業成長 15.7%。數據顯示 AI 與電子設備提供局部支撐，但內
需疲弱與房地產調整仍未見明顯改善，整體復甦動能偏緩，成長結構對少數產業的依賴度偏高。
科技產業方面，7 月市場焦點轉向半導體設備、AI 模型與記憶體的自主化，惟題材面成分仍高於

基本面。據媒體報導，中國已開始製造國產浸沒式 DUV 微影設備，首批系統預計供應中芯國際、華
虹半導體及長鑫科技 ， 但2026 年產量可能僅約 5 台 ， 且效能 、 可靠度與量產驗證仍明顯落後ASML，
短期象徵意義大於實際產能貢獻 。 後續能否降低成熟製程與部分多重曝光製程對進口設備的依賴 ， 仍
取決於良率與客戶端驗證結果，對國產蝕刻、薄膜沉積、檢測及零組件的實質拉動亦需時間觀察。
AI 軟體端，月之暗面於 7 月推出 Kimi K3，模型具 2.8 兆參數、100 萬 Token 上下文及原生視覺
理解能力，並採開放權重策略，反映中國大模型競爭已由低價推論延伸至模型能力與開發者生態。惟
開放權重的商業模式尚未證明可持續獲利，應用落地速度亦存在不確定性。若相關應用推展順利，或
有利國產雲端服務 、 資料中心 、 伺服器與推論晶片需求 ； 但模型效率提升同時可能壓抑算力採購規模 ，
市場對 AI 投資的檢視標準將轉向使用效率與變現能力。
記憶體方面 ， 長鑫科技正由一般DRAM 逐步向 DDR5、LPDDR5X 及 HBM3 延伸 ， 市場研究 「估

計」 其2026 年底 DRAM 月產能可能接近 35 萬片晶圓，惟該預估未經證實，實際良率與產品組合仍
待確認 。 國產DUV 若逐步導入其產線 ， 或可提高設備取得的自主性 ， 但導入時程與穩定度均具變數。
整體而言，8 月中國市場可留意半導體設備、國產記憶體、AI 模型、資料中心與工業自動化，惟評價
已部分反映預期，投資判斷宜以技術驗證、訂單落地與實際營收貢獻為依據，避免過度追逐自主替代
題材。
展望 8 月，政策環境預計仍以穩增長及培育新動能為主，但政策效果傳導有限，市場表現料將持
續分化。中國上半年新動能，包括高端製造、數位經濟與現代服務業，對經濟成長的貢獻超過四成；
第三季製造業雖有 AI 相關產品、綠色科技出口及政策支持，惟房地產、基礎建設投資及中小企業需
求疲弱仍是主要壓抑因素。

拉美市場：巴西通膨改善支撐市場表現，惟總統大選不確定性仍存
7 月全球股市普遍面臨回檔，然而拉美股市表現卻相對抗跌，主因巴西市場先前已因降息預期收
斂而出現修正，而近期巴西通膨數據及通膨預期出現改善，使市場對於巴西通膨問題的擔憂有所緩
解，也帶動巴西指數表現相對硬挺，並為整體拉美市場指數提供支撐。
7 月巴西通膨數據出現改善，主要來自食品與燃料價格回落。7 月巴西 IPCA-15 消費者物價指
數月增 0.06%，明顯低於市場預期的 0.22%，年增率亦由 6 月的 4.80% 降至 4.52%。其中，家庭
食品價格由 6 月月增 0.87% 轉為月減 1.14%；燃料價格整體月減 0.90%， 汽油價格月減 0.68%，

帶動整體通膨放緩，並降低市場對近期巴西物價再度急升的疑慮。
此外，本次巴西通膨數據改善亦反映在核心通膨與服務通膨上，使優於預期的數據更接近結構性
通膨改善。五種核心通膨指標平均月增 0.21%，年增率由 4.45% 降至 4.35%。同時，服務通膨月
增 0.41%，低於市場預期的 0.59%，年增率也由 6.25% 降至 5.95%；然而，勞力密集型服務年增
率仍由 7.06% 升至 7.30%，顯示通膨壓力尚未全面消退，後續仍需持續觀察。
另一方面，儘管巴西通膨數據改善 ， 仍未逆轉市場對巴西央行降息幅度受限的預期。截至 7 月 27
日的巴西央行每週市場調查顯示，2026 年市場通膨預期較前期下降 3 個基點至 5.12%，但 2027
年仍上升 2 個基點至 4.22%，2028 年亦連續第二週上升 2 個基點至 3.80%，2029 年則維持
3.50%；四個年度皆持續高於巴西央行 3.0%的通膨目標 。此外 ，市場對 2026、2027、2028 及 2029
年底巴西基準利率的預期，亦分別維持在 14.00%、12.00%、10.50%及 10.00%，顯示市場對通膨

的疑慮仍限制巴西利率正常化的速度與幅度。
除了通膨之外，巴西總統大選是影響拉美市場的另一項關鍵因素。巴西民調機構 Nexus 於 7 月
全國民調顯示，在總統大選第一輪中，魯拉以 42% 領先波索納洛的 33%，與 7 月中旬前次調查
的 42% 對 34% 大致相同；第二輪則為 47% 對 43%，魯拉仍領先 4 個百分點。兩輪調查均由
魯拉領先，顯示其整體勝率仍較高。此外，包含 Datafolha、Genial／Quaest 等民調皆顯示目前魯
拉贏面較高。由於魯拉並非市場偏好的候選人，其較高的連任機率可能壓抑巴西股市表現。因此，儘
管近期巴西通膨降溫有助支撐整體拉美市場行情，巴西選舉風險仍可能抵銷部分利多；同時，在美伊
衝突仍具不確定性的情況下，巴西降息行情亦料將受到壓抑，後市表現預期將落後於全球市場。

科技類股：CSP 廠商資本支出持續加碼，市場更加關注獲利轉化能力
全球科技產業的主要成長動能仍集中於 AI 基礎建設，四大雲端服務供應商資本支出均維持高檔
或進一步上修，顯示資料中心、AI 伺服器、網路設備及雲端容量的擴建週期尚未降溫。Microsoft、
Amazon 與 Google 的雲端業務均維持強勁成長，且訂單、產能需求與客戶使用量持續增加，反映企
業導入生成式 AI、主權 AI 及 AI 工廠的趨勢仍在深化。市場焦點也逐漸由單一 AI 晶片出貨，延伸至
高速網路、記憶體、CPU、電力與散熱等整體資料中心架構。只要 CSP 廠商未明顯下修資本支出，

AI 硬體供應鏈的訂單能見度便可望維持，NVIDIA、AMD 及相關半導體與設備廠商仍是本輪科技投
資循環的主要受惠者。
不過，市場對科技公司的評價標準正由投入多少資本支出轉向能否將投資轉化為營收、獲利與現
金流。Microsoft 在大幅增加資本支出的同時，營運現金流與自由現金流仍維持成長，Google 雲端
業務的獲利也明顯提升 ， 說明部分AI 投資已逐步進入商業化階段 ；Amazon 則受惠 AWS 成長加速，
雲端業務持續扮演主要獲利來源。相較之下，Meta 與特斯拉因 AI 研發、基礎建設及新事業投入快
速增加，面臨費用上升、利潤率下降與自由現金流承壓的問題。未來科技股表現可能進一步分化，市
場將更重視 AI 收入成長 、 產能利用率 、 投資回收期及折舊費用 ， 而非單純將高額資本支出視為利多。
從供應鏈角度來看，AI 需求正由 GPU 逐步擴散至 CPU、記憶體、晶圓代工、類比晶片與工業半
導體。Intel 資料中心與 AI 業務明顯加速，顯示 CPU 在 AI 伺服器的資料處理、系統控制與通用運算

架構中仍具重要地位。記憶體價格上升雖有利於相關製造商，但也推高 CSP 建置成本，促使雲端業
者重新檢視採購效率與系統配置。整體而言，科技產業有機會由少數 AI 核心企業領漲，逐步轉為伺
服器、網通、儲存、電源、散熱及工業半導體共同受惠的擴散型成長。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但高成長也伴隨高估值與高資本密集度風
險。NVIDIA 的 Blackwell 出貨、Rubin 平台時程、高階記憶體供應與中國市場需求，將是觀察 AI
硬體循環的重要指標；Apple、Meta 與特斯拉則需證明 AI 功能、新裝置及自動化應用能夠轉化為實
際收入。目前 CSP 訂單與雲端需求仍未出現明顯降溫，科技產業基本面維持正向，但隨著折舊、能
源、記憶體及研發成本陸續上升，企業獲利差異可能擴大。未來較具優勢的企業，將不只是擁有先進
技術或積極投入 AI， 而是能建立穩定商業模式 ， 並將AI 投資有效轉化為營收 、 利潤與現金流的公司。

能源類股：美伊衝突帶動油價波動，實體供應仍未見改善
7 月油價波動劇烈，主因美伊衝突與停火談判反覆。美伊在 7 月中旬恢復互相攻擊、美國重啟
對伊朗港口的海上封鎖，且荷莫茲海峽再度發生商船遇襲事件，帶動布蘭特原油一度重返每桶 100
美元以上；然而其後美方暫停攻擊並重啟外交談判後，油價又回落至約 80 美元，這顯示當前油價
變動主要反映市場對於衝突升降溫的預期，而非實際供應已恢復正常。
彭博分析指出，目前石油實物市場尚未出現明顯改善，荷莫茲海峽可觀察到的油輪數量仍遠低於
戰前，許多船隻通行時仍持續關閉船舶自動識別系統，且保險公司、租船人及船東也維持審慎態度，

顯示商業航運、海運保險、運費及出口流量均未恢復正常。航運資料公司 Windward 7 月 30 日的
船舶追蹤也顯示，荷莫茲海峽當日僅有 7 艘船舶進入波斯灣、4 艘離開，整體運量相較於戰前仍有
極大的落差，故一旦美伊談判受阻，地緣政治風險溢價仍有迅速回升的可能性。
另一方面，胡塞武裝宣布將封鎖沙烏地阿拉伯有關的船舶運輸，並對紅海曼德海峽通行發出威
脅，也加劇油價上行風險。彭博指出，沙烏地阿拉伯雖可利用東西輸油管，將原先在荷莫茲海峽受阻
的每日約 500 萬桶原油運往紅海沿岸的延布港，但經由紅海運往亞洲的船舶仍須通過曼德海峽，因
此原油出口風險只是由波斯灣轉移至紅海。船舶追蹤公司 Windward 截至 8 月 3 日的統計顯示，
7 月 20 日後曼德海峽船隻流量下降 22%， 油輪更減少 40%，顯示經紅海的替代路線仍面臨威脅。
然而 ， 高油價帶來的需求破壞也會限制油價上漲空間 。OPEC 7 月月報將 2026 年全球石油需求
量下修至每日 1.0594 億桶，需求年增量由每日 97 萬桶降至 78 萬桶；2027 年需求年增量雖上

修至每日 194 萬桶，但上修主因 2026 年基期較低的緣故，並非終端需求出現改善。
在此背景下，彭博研究將油價分為三種情境：若外交協議落地、荷莫茲航運與保險恢復正常，布
蘭特原油可能回到每桶 65 至 80 美元；若戰事僅暫停、實物供應持續受阻，則較符合每桶 90 至
110 美元的衝突僵局情境；若荷莫茲與曼德海峽同時受阻，油價可能升至每桶 140 美元以上。
展望 8 月，川普宣布將和伊朗展開新一輪談判，儘管隨即遭到伊朗否認，且荷莫茲海峽仍處於受阻
狀態 ， 然而我們認為隨著期中選舉時程逼近 ， 川普將可能暫緩對伊朗的行動 ， 藉此壓低油價挽救選情 ，
在此情境下能源類股將會轉趨區間震盪整理，目前中性看待後市表現。

身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：央行配置需求仍強，惟 ETF 資金流出壓抑短線表現
7 月金價保持在區間內震盪整理，儘管美元走強及美債殖利率攀升持續為金價帶來壓力，然而來
自全球央行的買盤也如我們先前所料的為金價帶來了有力的支撐。
世界黃金協會報告指出，2026 年第二季，全球黃金總需求為 1,269 噸，與 2025 年同期持平；
累計 2026 年上半年，全球黃金總需求年增 2%至 2,522 噸，需求金額創下 3,800 億美元新高。與此
同時 ，2026 年第二季倫敦金銀市場協會黃金午後定盤價平均為每盎司 4,506.29 美元 ， 雖較第一季下

跌 8%，但仍較 2025 年同期上漲 37%。這顯示第二季金價雖自高檔回落，但全球黃金需求並未同步
轉弱，尤其央行買盤回升更成為支撐市場的主要力量。
第二季全球央行合計購買 289 噸黃金，歷經第一季的放緩後，購金規模再次重新回到過去四年
的高檔水準。高盛分析則預估，全球央行 5 月購金量達到 81 噸，其中中國購買 48 噸，是當月最大
的可識別買家；經季節調整後，全球央行近三個月月均購金量達到 67 噸，約為 2022 年前每月 17
噸平均值的四倍 。 上述數據顯示 ， 即使在金價走勢低迷的狀況下，全球央行對於黃金的承接力道仍強 。
進一步觀察央行持續購金的原因，官方貨幣與金融機構論壇（OMFIF）2026 年針對 74 家央行
及 16 家公共退休與主權基金進行的調查顯示，儲備多元化仍是央行持有黃金最主要的原因。此外，
有 51%的受訪者將防範地緣政治風險列為考量，比例較 2024 年增加 11 個百分點；同時，79%的受
訪央行認為全球貨幣體系正朝向多極化發展 。調查結果顯示， 各國央行正逐步降低對單一儲備貨幣的

依賴，並增持黃金以因應制裁、資產凍結及貨幣體系分裂風險，由此也可見，本波全球央行買盤更接
近長期儲備調整，而非短線對於金價的追逐。
然而對於利率較為敏感的私人投資資金 ， 降低黃金配置趨勢仍持續為金價帶來明顯壓力。 世界黃
金協會數據顯示，2026 年第二季全球黃金 ETF 淨流出 45 噸，6 月單月再流出 89 億美元；其中，北
美黃金 ETF 上半年累計流出 77 億美元。造成北美資金流出的主因，在於聯準會態度偏向鷹派，加上
美伊衝突推升通膨疑慮，帶動實質利率與美元走高，進而提高持有黃金的機會成本。
展望後市，儘管短線上金價表現持續承壓，然而中長線看，當前市場已定價到明年底前 Fed 將
升息 2 碼的鷹派走勢，若後續美伊衝突有所緩和，使市場升息預期降溫，黃金 ETF 資金賣壓有望出
現改善， 屆時搭配央行長期買盤 ，黃金仍有望重啟新一波漲勢，故我們對黃金類股後市維持中性看法 。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：Fed 鷹派暫停推升長率，投資仍以 3~10 年期美債/投級債為主
FOMC 7/29 以 9 比 3 票將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.50%～3.75%。Hammack、Kashkari
及 Logan 三名委員反對維持利率不變，主張本次即升息 1 碼。會後聲明指出美國經濟活動仍以穩健
速度擴張，資本投資及生產力成長強勁，但通膨仍高於 2%目標，部分反映能源等供給衝擊。
Fed 主席 Warsh 強調，本次按兵不動並非聯準會行動遲緩或政策慣性，並重申不存在高於 2%
的 「軟性通膨目標」 。超過五年的高通膨不可能因單月物價小幅下降便獲得解決，聯準會將持續履行

物價穩定責任，必要時不會猶豫採取行動。華許同時指出，距離上次會議的 42 天內，美債殖利率曲
線各期限的名目與實質殖利率均明顯上升，部分升幅已位居近 20 年的前十分位數。他認為，聯準會
減少前瞻指引後，市場開始依據經濟數據自行調整價格。此番談話並非表示聯準會受制於市場，而是
暗示金融市場利率上升已產生部分緊縮效果，使本次不必立即升息。華許指出，AI 相關高科技設備
及軟體投資近四季增長率接近 20%，有助於製造業產出及未來生產力，但記憶體、邏輯晶片及相關
AI 基礎建設價格亦同步上升。FOMC 已開始討論，相關漲價究竟只是特定產業的供給衝擊，或可能
演變為更廣泛的通膨壓力。
債市對此次 FOMC 決議的反應呈現長端領跌、殖利率曲線明顯陡峭化。此一走勢顯示，市場雖
因 Fed 未立即升息而降低短端利率壓力，卻同步提高對能源通膨、財政供給、期限溢酬及政策可信
度的風險定價。利率期貨對 9 月升息的定價在決議後一度升至約 75%，稍後回落至約 6 成，代表市
場並未排除升息，而是等待後續通膨與就業數據確認。8 月沒有例行 FOMC 利率會議，債市定價將

更加依賴經濟數據。由於 Warsh 減少前瞻指引，單一數據對升息機率及短端殖利率的影響可能較過
去放大，預期 8 月殖利率仍將維持高檔震盪。
8 月債市的核心矛盾在於「殖利率已具吸引力，但長端風險尚未完全穩定」。我們認為偏高的殖
利率可提供較佳息收及價格緩衝；若油價維持高檔、核心通膨降溫停滯或美國公債供給增加， 20～
30 年期債券仍可能承受期限溢酬上升壓力。貝萊德亦指出，目前美債實質殖利率已具吸引力，但收
益機會仍應透過存續期間控制與券種選擇取得，而非全面延長年期。
在未來聯準會減少前瞻指引 、 利率路徑更依賴數據環境下 ， 市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將
提高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較
大波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供
給增加等風險。因此，建議債券布局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸
引力的息收，亦能降低短端政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

水 冷 散 熱 2026 年 營 收 維 持 成 長 趨 勢：  
水冷散熱產品有水冷板、 Manifold 、 CDU 、 Sidecar 。目前出貨以系統類的
Sidecar 為 主 ， 歸 類 於 基 礎 建 設； CDU 則 歸 類 於 電 源 類 與 零 組 件 。 水 冷 散 熱
2025 年 營 收 比 重 為 9%，台 達 電 Sidecar 產品 2025 年 市 佔 率 極 高，雖 有 競 爭
者 加 入 ， 但 整 體 市 場 需 求 持 續 增 長 ，2026 年 Sidecar 營 收 仍 將 維 持 成 長 趨
勢，預估 2026 年 水 冷 散 熱 營 收 比 重 為 12% 。

充 電 站，亦 出 貨 給 美 系 客 戶 測 試。SST 導 入 需 從 資 料 中 心 設 計 初 期 即 納 入 規
劃，預期  2~3 年 後 會 產 生 顯 著 的 營 收 貢 獻 ， 仍 持 續 改 良 產 品 規 格 中 。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我

務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT), the world’s largest PCB 
manufacturer by revenue, generated revenue of TWD182.5bn in 2025. 
Historically focused on consumer electronics and smartphones, the 
company is shifting towards higher-value AI-server PCBs (HLC/iHDI), 
optical-module PCBs (mSAP), and IC substrates (ABF/BT). These 
businesses accounted for 21.6% of its revenue in 1H26, up from 11.7% in 
2025. Management targets this ratio to reach c.25% in 2026 and c.30% in 
2027. To support this shift, ZDT has 22 fabs under construction and plans 
to build nine more, primarily for mSAP/HDI, HLC, and ABF substrates.

Highlights: ZDT’s “One ZDT strategy” is gradually bearing fruit. Since 
2018, management has pursued its “One ZDT strategy” to broaden the 
product portfolio – including HLC, substrates, mSAP/HDI, rigid-flex and 
SLP – by leveraging its strengths in FPCB and consumer HDI. This 
strategy is beginning to pay off, and management expects its fast-growing 
segments, including mSAP HDI for optical transceivers, AI-server HDI and 
ABF/BT substrates, to gain momentum from this year. Management 
expects revenue from these segments to increase by c.150% YoY and 
account for c.25% of total revenue (or c.TWD50bn) in 2026, and to further 
increase by 100% YoY to TWD100bn in 2027. ZDT targets these fast-
growing segments to reach 30% of its revenue mix in 2027 and c.45-50% 
in 2030. Given current progress, the 2030 target could be reached in 2028. 
For mSAP HDI, management expects the 800G/1.6T transceiver product 
mix to shift to 7:3 in 2027, from 5:5 in 2026, and aims to become the largest 
supplier of optical-transceiver HDI in 2027. In AI-server HDI, we expect the 
2 leading ASIC vendors to gradually shift from pure HLC to thick HDI 
boards – a combination of HLC and HDI – in their next-generation designs, 
with ZDT a potential beneficiary. This could drive c.3x growth in ZDT’s 
business in 2027.

Product mix to support gross margin expansion. Margin expansion is 
increasingly driven by a favourable product mix. Gross margin reached 
22.4% in 1H26 (vs. 16.5% in 1H25). Management indicated that optical-
transceiver HDIs currently have the highest gross margin, at over 40%, 
followed by IC substrates, with ABF margins potentially reaching 50-60% 
and BT margins around 30% as a long-term target. Server HLC margins 
are around 20-30%, while HDI margins should be higher given their greater 
process complexity. With margins in traditional businesses at c.20%, ZDT 
expects growth in the newer segments to drive further margin expansion.

Valuation: The stock is trading at 36/19x PER for 2026/27E based on 
Bloomberg consensus. It’s past 3-year PER range is 10-30x.

20 October 2026

Business transformation bears fruit

➢ Key beneficiary of growth in optical transceiver & AI server HDI demand  
➢ Aggressive substrate capacity strategy paying off in current conditions   
➢ Margin expansion on fast-growing segments (HDI and substrates)

Share price performance

### Page 3

Genius Electronic Optical (3406.TW)
27 October 2026 Citi Research
3
EBO: Solving AI Data Centers’ Connectivity
Bottlenecks
Expanded Beam Optical (EBO) technology uses lens arrays to expand and collimate
light between optical fibers, enabling transmission across a contactless interface.
Compared with conventional physical-contact connectors, EBO reduces sensitivity
to dust, cleaning requirements and connector wear, while enabling faster
installation and higher connection density. These benefits become increasingly

valuable as AI clusters require more fiber connections in difficult-to-access
locations, including optical backplanes. For Genius, this creates an opportunity to
extend its precision-optics expertise into infrastructure where reliability and
deployment efficiency directly affect customer economics.
Figure 1. 3M – EBO product demo Figure 2. 3M – EBO product demo
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission. © 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.

Source: Citi Research, 3M Source: Citi Research, 3M
Technology Requirements and Genius’s Manufacturing
Advantage
EBO relaxes some connector-interface tolerances, but manufacturing its optical
structures still requires precise lens geometry, consistent fiber-to-lens positioning,
dimensional stability and stringent optical-loss control. The competitive challenge
is reproducing that performance at high volumes with attractive yields. Genius’
established precision-optics manufacturing capabilities and production scale

therefore complement 3M’s proprietary ferrule technology, materials expertise and
customer relationships. Their collaboration supports commercialization and
capacity expansion, while 3M’s planned approximately 4.7% equity investment
strengthens strategic alignment. In our view, this provides Genius with a credible
route into optical connectivity, given its smartphone-lens expertise translates into
qualified, reliable production.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Genius Electronic Optical (3406.TW)
27 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Implications for Genius: Strategic Diversification, with
Earnings Following the Ramp
The partnership positions Genius as a manufacturing participant in 3M’s EBO
expansion, offering a potential growth engine beyond consumer-device optics. Our
interpretation is that technology qualification, production yields and capacity
readiness will matter most initially, with stronger revenue potential as deployments

accelerate from 2027. We estimate the revenue contribution to reach about
8%/28% in 2027 /2028 assuming around 30m/140m units based on 3M’s
addressable market estimate.
Muted smartphone outlook well anticipated, more optical
upside in AI
Genius’ near-term momentum is still highly relying on smartphone order flow. Its
peer, Largan, has indicated a muted outlook for the smartphone industry as the
peak production ramp for newly launched models has passed; we expect Genius’

revenue to decline seasonally in 4Q26. However, another wave of base-model
smartphone launches in 1Q27 should help support demand during what is typically
its seasonally slower period at the start of next year. Given its steady market share
gain, we expect a stable GM ahead. While Genius is also navigating the
opportunities in CPO and PMLA for FAU, we believe it takes time and the EBO
contribution will provide more substantial upside for the next two years. We

slightly lower our earnings projection for 2026/2027 but lift 2028 estimate by 6%,
given better visibility for its cooperation with 3M. We raise our TP to NT$1,380 from
NT$1,050 previously (higher PER of 33x of our 2027 /28 EPS projection vs 25x
previously). 33x PER is above the high end of its 5-year historical range as we
believe the high growth optical opportunity in AI arena and 3M's strategic
cooperation should further reinforce Genius' position in capturing the expanding

optical connector markets, leading to better growth and re-rating potential for
Genius.

### Page 8

Genius Electronic Optical (3406.TW)
27 October 2026 Citi Research
8
Genius Electronic Optical
Company description
Genius is a leading lens provider. It was established in 1989 and listed on
TWSE in 2005. Its revenue increased from NT$4.5bn in 2010 to NT$23.2bn in
2024. The revenue breakdown for Genius in 2023 was 73% smartphone lens
and 27% others.
Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Genius. We expect Genius to see resilient sales
momentum driven by stable lens sales in iPhone business. Moreover, we see

its new development in EBO business to provide strong upside potential in
the longer term.
Valuation
Our target price for Genius of NT$1,380 is based on 33x 2027 /28 EPS. We are
using 2027 /28 as the valuation base year as we believe the new business in
optical connector will be a new catalyst starting from next year. 33x PER is
above the high end of its 5-year historical range as we believe the high growth
optical opportunity in AI arena will lead better growth and re-rating and 3M's

strategic cooperation further reinforces Genius' position in capturing the
expanding optical connector markets. Genius used to trade based on
smartphone product cycle and the growth has been stagnant. With the new
optical connector opportunity, we view it should be resuming strong growth
ahead.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean the stock fails to achieve our target price
include: lower-than-expected smartphone sell-through; lower iPhone share

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

M
Update
Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 GM Dragged by Inventory
Write-off
Despite the secular tailwinds of increasing power consumption
in AI clusters, we think near-term sentiment toward Delta could
remain lackluster post weaker 3Q26 results.
3Q26 earnings fell short on lower GM:
• Off revenue of NT$197,059mn (+8% QoQ/+31% YoY), GM contracted  by
1.6ppt QoQ to 34.1% (vs. MSe/consensus at 35.7%/36.2%), which was
dragged 2ppt lower by the inventory write-off

• Opex was well controlled at NT$34,535mn (opex ratio 17.5%), lower than our
estimate of NT$36,983mn (opex ratio 18.4%) and consensus of NT$37,129mn
(opex ratio 18.0%)
• Operating profit came in at NT$32,620mn (+7% QoQ/+31% YoY; OPM
16.6%), 7% lower  than MSe of NT$34,992mn (OPM 17.4%) and 13% lower
than consensus of NT$37,677mn (OPM 18.2%).
• With non-op gains of NT$1,046mn (including FX gain of NT$415mn), net
profit was NT$23,495mn (-7% QoQ/+26% YoY; EPS NT$9.04), 8%/15% short

of MSe/consensus at NT$25,554mn (EPS NT$9.84)/NT$27,611mn (EPS NT
$10.40).
Our view:
• We believe near-term sentiment toward the stock will stay soft, though the
weak financial performance in 3Q26 was mainly impacted by component
supply tightness
• We believe Delta should grow its revenue by 10% QoQ with margin
expansion in 4Q26, and will continue to benefit from the increasing power
consumption of AI clusters in 2027
• Earnings call at 3pm HKT on Oct 29th  (details here)

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28 EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 63% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.

n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
3Q Result Miss, Expecting Recovery in 4Q26
Delta’s 3Q earnings and GM were below expectation due to component shortages
and delays in data center deployment. GM declined 1.6ppts QoQ to 34.1%,
primarily reflecting inventory provisions equivalent to 1.7% of revenue, driven by
supply chain mismatches, customer delivery postponements, and product
specification adjustments. Nevertheless, OPM remained resilient at 16.6%, broadly

flat QoQ and YoY, supported by operating leverage.
Management indicated that AI-related businesses across its four divisions now
account for approximately 50% of consolidated revenue, highlighting Delta’s
increasing exposure to AI infrastructure investment. Management remains
confident in Delta’s competitive positioning, particularly its integrated capabilities
spanning power conversion, power distribution, thermal management, and
infrastructure solutions. Although the industry continues to face component

shortages, especially for certain power semiconductor devices, Delta believes its
supply chain management capabilities compare favorably to competitors. The
company also plans to establish a major manufacturing facility in Penang,
Malaysia, leveraging the region’s established infrastructure supply chain and
engineering talent.
Figure 1. Delta - 3Q26 earnings review
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus

HVDC Adoption and SOFC Commercialization Create
Additional Long- T erm Growth Opportunities
Delta expects the transition toward high-voltage DC power architecture to
accelerate as AI rack power density continues to increase. The company began
shipping ±400V HVDC solutions in 3Q26, while NVIDIA is promoting 800VDC
architectures for next-generation AI infrastructure. Management believes HVDC
adoption will eventually become widespread given its potential to improve power

conversion efficiency and reduce distribution losses, although standardization,
installation requirements, and shortages of qualified electrical workers could
constrain the near-term rollout. Beyond power conversion, Delta is developing
solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC) solutions to address growing demand for distributed
power generation. The company plans to begin commercial production in 2027,
with an initial manufacturing line providing approximately 100MW of annual

capacity, primarily supporting customer qualification and pilot installations. Initial

### Page 4

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research

customers include domestic utility and industrial users, alongside several overseas
customers. Management emphasized that SOFC shipments will remain limited
initially as the company prioritizes product reliability and qualification.
4Q26 Recovery Expected as Inventory Normalizes, with
Positive Growth Outlook for 2027
Management expects revenue to increase sequentially in 4Q26 as delayed
customer deliveries resume and inventory begins to normalize. Gross margin

should improve from 3Q26 levels, although management remained cautious given
potential year-end inventory adjustments. The 3Q26 inventory provisions largely
reflected customer-specific products rather than structural obsolescence, and
some provisions could potentially be reversed following successful deliveries.
Nevertheless, management acknowledged that inventory buffers will likely remain
structurally higher than historical levels to accommodate volatile AI infrastructure

deployment schedules. Raw material costs have increased approximately 2% year-
to-date, prompting ongoing pricing negotiations with customers, with limited
recovery expected in 4Q26 and a more meaningful impact in 2027. Operating
expenses will continue increasing in absolute terms to support R&D and capacity
expansion. Management remains positive on 2027 revenue growth, although it did
not provide quantitative guidance. Separately, management expects no near-term

shipment disruption from the ongoing Vicor intellectual property litigation.
We lower earnings projection on power shortages, labor constraints, financing
conditions, and regulatory uncertainties could create quarterly shipment volatility,
but we believe its multiyear AI infrastructure growth cycle intact. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$1,715 (based on lower PER of 35x of 2027 EPS, from 40x previously)

Bull/Bear: Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 7

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Delta Electronics
Company description
Delta was established in 1975 and is the world's leading computer switching
power supply manufacturer. Its business is divided into: 1) Power Electronics
(59% of 2022 revenue), including Switching Power Supply, Components,
Display and EV Solution; 2) Infrastructure (27% of revenue), including Wind
power, Telecom power, UPS, EV Infrastructure, Networking; 3) Automation

(14% of revenue), including Industrial Automation (IA), Building Automation;
and 4) Others ( <1% of revenue).
Investment strategy
We rate Delta Electronics shares as Buy due to its strong execution and the
ability to expand margin through favorable product mix shift to AI power and
thermal solutions. We believe EV business slowdown is in the price, and we
expect margins to improve driven by AI solution spec upgrades and expanding
business scale. Delta is now positioning itself as an infrastructurer enabler

rather than a pure component supplier, which deepens its key position in the
AI supply chain.
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$1,715 is based on 35x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since
2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with

customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly
than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

股價相對大盤走勢

長榮航太 (2645 TT)  Trading Buy

預期波音 737 系列交機速度將略為放緩。  
投資建議 ：雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量
波音將維持 737 系列月均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太
的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航太 Trading Buy 的投資建
議。  
波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造
的營運預估： 波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會
上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時
間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到 2H27 才會上升至 52
架」，使得長榮航太的製造部門復甦仍需等待一段時間，故研
究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
3Q26 先前的維修漲價陸續貢獻，營運維持高檔： 3Q26 長榮航
太自 2H24 起陸續針對維修業務進行漲價後，加上全球機隊持
續供不應求，增加維修機率與維修產值，惟製造部門雖然波音
已將 737 系列交機上升至 47 架 /月，但短期拉貨力道仍有限，
使得長榮航太的製造業務與轉投資公司營運維持低檔，營運仍
以維修業務為主， 3Q26 營收 52.75 億元， QoQ+1.02% ，預估稅
後純益 8.31 億元， QoQ+10.34% ， EPS 2.22 元。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營
運新動能： 2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機
的逐年擴張之外，在新訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，
預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待後續取得特殊製程認證後，
2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單也有望成為長榮
航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ，EPS 10.04 元。

列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到

2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營運新動能：  
2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機的逐年擴張之外，在新
訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待

YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ， EPS 10.04 元。

均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航
太 Trading Buy 的投資建議。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3481 群創光電(INNOLUX)

報告日期： 2026/10/27 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 637 億，毛利率 14.6% ，營利率 2.6% ，EPS 0.57
元。受惠於非顯示器比重持續提升 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。  
 公司今年處分三座廠房，累計獲利約為 200 億元，目前資產處
分已告一段落 ， 暫無進一步出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP
投資調整既有廠區資源。  
 因上半年提前拉貨以及零組件漲價影響，消費型顯示器需求動
能相對保守；半導體事業部分， Chip-first 製程已進入量產，
每月出貨量約 4,000 萬片，而 RDL-first 與 TGV （玻璃基板）
製程部分，目前正與客戶緊密合作推進，預期玻璃基板封裝產
品將於 2028 年推出。

一、 2Q26 毛利率達 14.6% ，EPS 0.57 元 
群創 2Q26 營收 637 億，QoQ -4.6% ，YoY +13.1% ， 而獲利方面，
2Q26 營業毛利 92.7 億， QoQ -3.6% ，YoY +96.8% ，毛利率
14.6% ，QoQ +0.1ppts ，YoY +6.2ppts ，營業淨利 16.3 億，營利
率 2.6% ，2Q26 業外淨利 16.3 億，稅後淨利 46.5 億，單季稅後
EPS 0.57 元。而就上半年來看， 1H26 消費型顯示器佔整體營收
45% ，平均毛利率為 6%至 10% ；商用顯示器佔 13% ，平均毛利
率 16% 至 20%； 非顯示器領域佔42%， 平均毛利率11% 至 15% ，
受惠於商用、非顯示器比重提升， 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。

二、資產處分告一段落，後續聚焦 FOPLP 產能  
為充實營運資金、活化公司旗下資產，群創今年處分三座廠房， 3
月將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59 億元 )，以及
將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )。公司 4 月再
度宣布，將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價 148.5 億元售予日月光
投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年群創認列的售廠總
獲利約為 197.59 億元。公司表示，目前資產處分已告一段落，暫
無進一步大規模出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP 投資調整既有
廠區資源。

三. 消費性面板短期保守，持續朝高階封裝領域布局  
公司公告 9 月營收 199.95 億， MoM +6.9% ，YoY +0.7% ，3Q26
營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
投資評等：區間操作 
目標價位：42.8~54.6 元

度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027
全年營收 2659 億元，YoY +8.5% ，毛利率 14.3% ，營利率 3.1% ，
稅後淨利 892 億，全年稅後 EPS 1.12 元，每股淨值 29.51 元。

四. 投資建議為 42.8~54.6 元區間操作  
公司持續投入半導體 FOPLP 領域，聚焦轉型、資產活化題材，但
考量到 4Q26 至明年上半年面板事業展望保守 ， 半導體事業部分，
後續仍須持續觀察客戶認證、量產進度及營運貢獻狀況。目前股
價交易於 2027 BVPS 約 1.64 倍 PBR 水準，以公司過往 PBR 介
於 0.4x~2.5x 區間水準，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價為
42.8~54.6 元(1.45x~1.85 x 2027 BVPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
群創光電 (INNOLUX) 為全球第三大面板廠，主要產品為大尺寸 (大
於 9 吋)與中小尺寸 (9 吋以下 )與延伸技術之薄膜電晶體液晶顯示
器(TFT-LCD) 相關產品，大尺寸產品應用於液晶電視、公共看板、
桌上型監視器、筆記型電腦與工業控制等；中小尺寸則為平板電
腦、可攜式影音播放器、車載、航空船舶、智能家居及手機、穿
戴裝置等應用，也可選擇性搭配各類型觸控面板；另針對特殊使
用需求，也提供應用於醫療、軍用、教育與電子紙應用之產品。
集團總部位於苗栗縣竹南鎮，台灣生產基地位於竹南、南科、台
南、高雄等地，另有海外生產基地則包含中國深圳、中國寧波、
中國南京、中國南海、荷蘭等地。 2025 年公司營收占比大宗消費
品(Commodity) 55% ；非大宗消費品 (Non-commodity) 16% ；非
顯示器領域群 (Non-display) 29% 。公司 NB 客戶為 DELL，液晶電
視客戶包括 Panasonic 、Sharp 、Sony、Toshiba 、Samsung 、TCL
等；手機及平板電腦客戶為 Samsung 、Amazon 、Apple 及華為；
車用面板客戶包括 BMW、福 特、General Motors 、 特斯拉等車廠。
國內主要競爭廠商為友達光電、瀚宇彩晶，國外廠商則包括

2. 群創連售三廠房，預估獲利近 200 億元 
為充實營運資金並挹注未來發展動能，群創近期積極活化旗下資
產。繼 3 月先後將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59
億元 )，以及將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )
後，群 創 於15 日再度宣布 ， 將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價148.5
億元售予日月光投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年以
來，預估公司可望認列售廠總獲利達 197.59 億元。

4 
二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 需求推動，FOPLP 與玻璃基板市場 2030 年將突破 80 億美元 
根據  Counterpoint Research 報告，受惠於 AI 與高效能運算
(HPC) 強勁需求 ， 全球扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP) 與玻璃基板市場
規模，預計將從 2024 年的 6.5 億美元飆升至 2030 年的 81 億美
元以上，其中 AI 與 HPC 應用將佔 FOPLP 總營收的 45.6% 。相較
於傳統有機基板，玻璃基板具備高互連密度、尺寸穩定與抗翹曲
等優勢，能支援次世代小晶片 (Chiplet) 架構與大型 AI 處理器，吸
引台積電、 Intel 、三星電機、日月光及力成等大廠積極擴展產能
與研發。區域佈局上，預計 2030 年台灣、日本與中國大陸將囊括
全球 84.8% 的面板級封裝產能。儘管目前仍面臨面板尺寸標準化
與玻璃穿孔 (TGV) 製程等量產挑戰，但在產業鏈合作推動下，整體
市場前景依舊十分樂觀。

圖三、 2024-2030 年各地區 FOPLP 面板產能預估

資料來源： Counterpoint

2. 零組件成本壓力與需求衰退交織，10 月面板價格全面持平 
根據 TrendForce 預估，進入第 4 季後，受上游零組件成本上漲壓
力遽增及終端採購需求衰退雙重影響， 10 月 TV、MNT 與 NB 三
大面板價格預估將全面持平。 TV 面板部分，雖有原廠調控產能並
喊漲，但主要品牌第 4 季採購需求季減約 5%，短期難接受漲價；
而 MNT 面板需求持續減弱，惟成本壓力使面板廠無讓步空間，買
賣雙方對持平具共識，後續將視 TV 面板能否成功漲價而定；另
外， NB 面板則因前六大品牌第 4 季採購需求恐季減近 30% ，出
貨動能嚴重萎縮，在成本高漲且降價無助爭取訂單的情況下，雙
方亦達成維持價格穩定的共識。

투자의견 BUY

목표주가 500,000

현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상 
(단위: 십억원, %)

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

현재주가 
(26.10.02) 
1,354,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6 공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22

Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% Y oY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% Y oY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

LG에너지솔루션

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다.

### Page 1

Q3 亚⻢逊营收同⽐增 12% 、略低于分析师预期， EPS 同⽐不增反降 3% ，经营利润率逼近
14% 创历史新⾼； AWS 收⼊增速近五年最⾼、预计明后年均增超 40% ； Q3 线上销售仅增
2% 、增速三年半最低，⼴告收⼊较预期低 6% ；资本⽀出翻⼀倍多⾄ 773 亿美元，较预期⾼
逾 30% ；⾃由现⾦流负 424 亿美元、为 Q2 的 5.6 倍； Q4 营收和利润指引中值低于预期。公
司维持今年资本⽀出指引 2200 亿美元不变。股价盘后⼀度跌超 10% 。
摘要：
亚⻢逊三季度财报呈现明显的两⾯性：核⼼经营利润强于预期、备受关注的云计算业务 AWS 保
持⾼速增⻓，但核⼼业务电商增⻓乏⼒，公司总营收和 EPS 盈利均逊于华尔街预期，且 AI 相关资
本开⽀继续维持⾼位，令市场对⾃由现⾦流和投资回报周期的担忧升温。
美东时间 29 ⽇周四美股盘后，亚⻢逊公布，截⾄ 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇的今年第三季度，公司净销售
额同⽐增⻓ 12% ⾄ 2019.4 亿美元，略低于分析师预期的约 2022.6 亿美元；稀释后每股收益

（ EPS ）同⽐下降约 3.1% ⾄ 1.89 美元，分析师预期同⽐微增 0.5% ⾄ 1.96 美元；⽽经营利润同⽐
增 61.5% ⾄ 280.8 亿美元，较市场预期⾼逾 7% ，经营利润率升⾄ 13.9% ，为公司史上最⾼⽔平。
财报最⼤亮点仍是 AWS 。三季度 AWS 净销售额同⽐增⻓ 39.3% ⾄ 459.8 亿美元，较分析师预期⾼
1.3% ，创将近五年来最⾼单季增速，季末年化收⼊运转率达 1840 亿美元，环⽐⼆季度末时增⻓
8.9% 。 CEO Andy Jassy 在财报中强调， AWS 在功能和基础设施上的投⼊正在奏效，并预计
2027 年和 2028 年 AWS 收⼊增速都将超过 40% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

相⽐ AWS ，三季度线上销售仅同⽐增⻓ 2% ，创 2023 年初以来最低单季增速。亚⻢逊对假⽇季、
第四季度销售的指引中值逊于分析师预期也显⽰，其线上业务短期内恐难迅速回暖。
虽然 AWS 收⼊继续劲增，但市场更在意 “ 代价 ” 。亚⻢逊三季度购置物业和设备的资本⽀出⾼达
772.6 亿美元、约为去年同期的 1.2 倍，较市场预期⾼逾 34% ；⾃由现⾦流连续第⼆季录得负值，
从去年同期的流⼊ 147.9 亿美元转为流出 423.6 亿美元，流出规模远超⼆季度。公司还维持约
2200 亿美元的 2026 年全年现⾦资本⽀出指引不变，并未释放 AI 投⼊放缓的信号。
财报公布后，周四本已收跌超 4% 的亚⻢逊股价盘后进⼀步跳⽔，盘后跌幅⼀度超过 10% 。
营收略低于预期，但经营利润率创历史新⾼
从整体收⼊看，亚⻢逊三季度净销售额 2019.4 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 12% ；若剔除汇率影响，增速
为 13% 。
亚⻢逊特别提⽰，今年会员⽇ Prime Day 在公司的多数重要市场地区均提前⾄⼆季度，⽽ 2025 年

Prime Day 完全落在三季度，因此今年三季度同⽐增速受到时点错位影响；若剔除这⼀因素，三
季度收⼊同⽐增速将⾼出约 400 个基点。
利润端则明显更强。亚⻢逊三季度经营利润为 280.8 亿美元，较去年同期的 174.2 亿美元⼤增
61% ，经营利润率从去年同期的 9.7% 提升⾄ 13.9% 。这意味着，即便收⼊略低于预期，公司在
履约、销售营销、管理费⽤等⽅⾯的效率改善，仍然⽀撑了创纪录的经营利润率。

### Page 3

净利润为 206.9 亿美元，低于去年同期的 211.9 亿美元；稀释后 EPS 为 1.89 美元，同⽐下降 3% 。
这 ⼀ 同 ⽐ 下 滑 很 ⼤ 程 度 上 与 去 年 同 期 的 ⾮ 经 营 性 ⾼ 基数 有 关 ——2025 年 三 季 度 亚 ⻢ 逊 因
Anthropic 投资确认了 95 亿美元税前收益，⽽今年同期没有类似收益，其他收⼊ / 费⽤项⽬从去年
同期的正 101.9 亿美元转为负 7 亿美元。
换⾔之，这份财报不是经营层⾯的利润失速，⽽是收⼊和每股收益略低于预期、叠加⾮经营性
项⽬和资本开⽀压⼒，削弱了投资者对短期回报的信⼼。
AWS 继续加速： AI 需求⽀撑云业务，但市场也在追问投⼊回报
AWS 是三季度最亮眼的业务线。该部⻔净销售额 459.8 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 39% ，⾼于分析师预期
的约 454 亿美元，同⽐增速较⼆季度的约 37% 进⼀步加快，继续创 2021 年四季度以来最⾼增速；
AWS 经营利润 177.7 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 55% ，经营利润率为 38.7% ，⾼于去年同期的 34.6% 。

从收⼊结构看，三季度线上销售额 685 亿美元，贡献亚⻢逊三分之⼀的净销售额， AWS 销售额占
总额的 23% ，⾼于去年同期的 18% ，但其贡献了亚⻢逊整体经营利润的约 63% 。这说明亚⻢逊
的利润池仍⾼度依赖云业务，⽽ AWS 的重新加速，对⽀撑集团估值⾄关重要。
财报披露， AWS 近期与 Meta 、英伟达、 OpenAI 、 Salesforce 、甲⻣⽂、⾼通、 Verizon 等签署
了新协议。 Amazon Bedrock 继续快速增⻓，财富 500 强企业在 Bedrock 上的⽀出同⽐接近 7 倍，
整体 token 量同⽐增⻓近 17 倍； Bedrock AgentCore 已有数万名客⼾使⽤，三季度部署的智能体
数量超过前三个季度总和的两倍。
Jassy 对 AWS 前景的表态也⾮常激进。他称 AWS 三季度年化收⼊运⾏率已达 1840 亿美元，并预
计 2027 年和 2028 年 AWS 收⼊增速均超过 40% 。他还表⽰，历史上从未出现过万亿美元收⼊规模

### Page 4

的企业，⽽亚⻢逊未来 5 到 10 年可能拥有两个这样的业务。
但这也是市场分歧所在： AWS 的增⻓说明 AI 投⼊正在转化为需求，但⾼增⻓同时需要巨额算
⼒、数据中⼼和芯⽚投资。投资者并⾮否认 AWS 复苏，⽽是在重新评估这种增⻓需要消耗多少
现⾦流。
AI 资本开⽀成为股价压⼒核⼼：⾃由现⾦流⼤幅转负
资本开⽀是这份财报引发市场负⾯反应的关键。
三季度，亚⻢逊购置物业和设备⽀出为 772.6 亿美元，远⾼于去年同期的 351 亿美元，远超市场
预期的 574.7 亿美元。过去 12 个⽉，公司购置物业和设备净额达到 2106.6 亿美元，同⽐增⻓
82% 。亚⻢逊明确表⽰，这⼀增⻓主要反映了⼈⼯智能相关投资。
现⾦流表显⽰，过去 12 个⽉亚⻢逊经营现⾦流为 1683 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 29% ；但⾃由现⾦流为
流出 423.6 亿美元、流出规模约为⼆季度 76 亿美元的 5.6 倍，⽽去年同期为流⼊ 147.9 亿美元。也
就是说，公司主营业务造⾎能⼒仍在增强，但 AI 基础设施投⼊的速度更快，已经吞噬并反转了⾃

由现⾦流。
资产负债表也体现出扩张压⼒。截⾄三季度末，亚⻢逊现⾦及现⾦等价物为 557.8 亿美元，低于
去年底的 868.1 亿美元；⻓期债务升⾄ 1731.3 亿美元，⽽去年底为 656.5 亿美元。三季度公司新
增⻓期债务融资 481 亿美元，前三季度新增⻓期债务融资 1151 亿美元。
对成⻓型科技公司⽽⾔，资本开⽀本⾝并⾮负⾯，尤其是在 AWS 增速重新上⾏的背景下。但当
三季度资本开⽀⼤幅超预期、⾃由现⾦流显著转负，同时公司⼜维持全年约 2200 亿美元现⾦资
本开⽀预期时，市场⾃然会担⼼： AI 投⼊何时进⼊回报期？未来⼏年是否还会持续挤压现⾦流？
北美和国际业务：收⼊受 Prime Day 错位影响，利润改善明显
零售相关业务表⾯上增速不强，但利润端改善较为明显。
北美业务三季度净销售额 1128.5 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 6% ，略低于市场预期的约 1133.9 亿美元；经
营利润 87.2 亿美元，较去年同期的 47.9 亿美元⼤增 82% ，经营利润率从 4.5% 提升⾄ 7.7% 。

国际业务净销售额 431.1 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 5% ，也略低于预期；若剔除汇率影响，增速为 8% 。
该部⻔经营利润 15.8 亿美元，⾼于去年同期的 12 亿美元，经营利润率从 2.9% 提升⾄ 3.7% 。

### Page 5

亚⻢逊解释称， Prime Day 时点变化压低了三季度收⼊增速。在线商店收⼊ 684.7 亿美元，同⽐
仅增⻓ 2% ；第三⽅卖家服务收⼊ 464.9 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 9% ；实体店收⼊ 57.1 亿美元，同⽐增
⻓ 2% 。全球付费商品数量同⽐增⻓ 12% ，全球运输成本同⽐增⻓ 11% ，显⽰订单量仍在增⻓，
但收⼊结构受到促销时点影响。
因此，零售业务不能简单理解为需求恶化。更准确地说，三季度零售收⼊表现不如市场预期，
但利润率改善和配送效率提升仍然延续。公司称， Prime 会员三季度收货速度创历史同期最快，
超过 15 亿件商品实现当⽇或次⽇送达。
⼴告业务增⻓放缓，是财报中另⼀短板
⼴告业务三季度表现低于预期，是除资本开⽀外另⼀个压制情绪的因素。
亚⻢逊⼴告服务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 11% ⾄ 196.4 亿美元，较分析师预期收⼊ 208.7 亿美元低约 6% ；剔
除汇率影响增⻓ 12% 。这⼀增速相⽐此前多个季度 20% 以上的增⻓明显放缓，也低于市场预期
的约 208.7 亿美元。

公司表⽰，⼴告收⼊受到 Prime Day 时点以及其他若⼲时间因素影响，并预计四季度⼴告收⼊同
⽐增速将回升⾄⾼⼗位数区间。如果这⼀指引兑现，三季度⼴告降速可能更多是时点扰动，⽽
⾮⼴告业务结构性放缓。
不过，市场短期难免会更敏感。⼴告是亚⻢逊近年来利润率更⾼、增⻓更快的业务之⼀，⼀旦
增速放缓，就会削弱投资者对零售⽣态商业化能⼒的信⼼。相⽐之下，订阅服务表现较稳，三
季度收⼊ 140.1 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 11% ，⾼于市场预期的约 136.9 亿美元。
四季度指引不算差，但营收和利润中值低于预期
亚⻢逊给出的四季度指引总体处于华尔街预期附近，但并未带来⾜够惊喜。
公司预计四季度净销售额在 2400 亿⾄ 2470 亿美元之间，同⽐增⻓ 12% ⾄ 16% ，其中包含约 60 个
基点的汇率不利影响。该营收区间的中值为 2435 亿美元，略低于分析师预期的 2445.2 亿美元，
但预期值落在指引区间内。
更关键的是经营利润指引。亚⻢逊预计四季度经营利润为 285 亿⾄ 335 亿美元，⽽市场预期约

331 亿美元。换⾔之，公司指引上限仅略⾼于共识预期，中点 310 亿美元则低于预期。
这解释了为何股价反应如此剧烈：三季度营收和 EPS 均⼩幅不及预期，⼴告收⼊低于预期，四
季度利润指引中点偏弱，同时资本开⽀⼤幅超预期且全年 AI 投⼊计划维持⾼位。 AWS 的强劲增

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book 1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings hav e generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than tw o-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.” 2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market. 2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of inv estment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractiv e in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market mo ves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting go vernment
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff

* “Pop the Champagne,” Portfolio Insights, Chief Investment Office, August 2026.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and
